Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any bosons module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 212. The projective resolution has length at most 120 with Betti numbers bounded by 2.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_212(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 212. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^120(S, F) is trivial. QED.
